# P01–P12 hardening re-audit

- **Revision audited:** `a53a2bfd7328fbff16f32a79c956e5dc6a0c9a94` (`a53a2bf`, the main merge commit of 2026-09-23 07:31 IST). This is HEAD of the read-only worktree `recoup-wt-paudit` (branch `paudit`).
- **Date:** 2026-09-23.
- **Status:** audit report only.
  - No production file was edited.
  - Nothing was committed, pushed, stashed or deployed.
  - No real provider was called, and no secret or environment value was read.

## Method

**Three passes per item.** For each of P01–P12:

1. **Auditor.** An independent pass by an agent that did not write the code. It gave each sub-requirement one of the mission §15 classes: `already_fixed`, `still_present`, `not_reproduced`, `changed_scope_with_evidence` or `externally_blocked`. It reproduced defects with throwaway `zz_paudit_*` tests.
2. **Skeptic.** It re-ran the auditor's repros, removed each claimed fix in a throwaway copy (mutation testing) to check that a committed test then fails, and looked for work the auditor missed.
3. **Section writer.** It applied the skeptic's corrections, re-ran the proving test files at `a53a2bf`, re-read the cited lines and wrote the section.

**Proof bar.** `already_fixed` requires a committed test that fails when the fix is removed. Code that behaves correctly but has no such test is classified `still_present` (test gap) or `not_reproduced`, as each section explains.

**External limits.** The dev provider keys are placeholders (D83, D202), and production is not authorized (D136). Anything that needs a real provider, production or a paid key is `externally_blocked`. Its local mechanism was still verified, with `fetch` stubbed at the provider boundary.

**Models.** Every pass ran on Claude Opus 5.5 (`claude-opus-5-5`, self-reported): 12 auditors, 12 skeptics, 12 section writers and the assembler of this report.

**Assembly.**
- The P01–P12 sections below are included verbatim.
- The summary table and the consolidated backlog at the end are built from those sections.
- 11 duplicate backlog items are merged. Every merged row keeps all of its source IDs.

## Summary

Every item is still open (`still_present`). The fixes from the original baseline mostly hold, and tests prove them. What remains is mostly Mission-2 regressions of the same kind, missing regression tests, and ops and docs work.

- **In the sections:** 175 open backlog items.
- **After merging 11 duplicates:** 164 items: **3 high, 54 medium, 106 low and 1 info**.
- **Externally blocked:** 10 of the 164.

Counts in the last column are per section, before merging. Ext. = externally blocked.

| Item | Primary class | What remains | Open items (H / M / L; ext.) |

That one path causes two enumeration oracles, mails codes to tombstoned accounts, and leaves stray auth rows.<br>**Low:** a Settings toggle that is wrong while the address is suppressed; consent and unsubscribe copy that contradicts behaviour; test gaps (global mail cap, CSPRNG, reset session revocation); a false reply to a stale unsubscribe token; code collisions.<br>**Blocked:** the live mail rehearsal (P01-7). | 0 / 2 / 8; 1 |

## Assembly notes (cross-item)

These notes come from reading the sections side by side, plus the assembler's own read-only checks. The sections themselves are left verbatim.

1. **Newer main.** Sections spot-checked later `origin/main` commits read-only where they say so (`1ba15d8`, `364ac39`, `6a2a1fc`, `b6f5e1a`).
   - At assembly time the local `origin/main` ref is `5446551`. That is 27 commits past `a53a2bf` and 2 past `b6f5e1a`: `924794a` (docs, D222–D237) and `5446551` (M20 wave-2 foundation, 53 files).
   - `5446551` changes `convex/account.ts`, `drafts.ts`, `recovery.ts`, `replies.ts` and `isolationM1.test.ts`. No tests were re-run against it. A read-only diff scan found no change to the defect lines cited for:
     - P02-OW-1/OW-3/SK-1 (the resend re-read, `suppressQueuedMail`, `sendStatus`);
     - P05-OW1 (the claims read without `.order`);
     - P08-W2 (the isolation glob);
     - P08-W3 (`senderMismatch` leading to `promised_credit`);
     - P10-MW-2 (`extract()` in `drafts.generate`).
   - The new tables `packets` and `submissions` are added to export, purge and the isolation probes.
   - `recovery.summary` now also reads up to `PACKETS_PER_CLAIM` + `SUBMISSIONS_PER_CLAIM` rows for each manual-channel claim. The D201 budget that the P05-OW1 fix must re-measure has therefore moved.
2. **P11 count.** P11 row 17 cites `convex/testing/checkRulePacks.test.ts` as 30/30. At `a53a2bf` the file has 31 tests, and all 31 pass, as P12 row 7 says.
3. **P11-MW1 decision number.** P11-MW1 says the flake fix is "D231, not yet recorded". On `origin/main` (`924794a`), D231 records only that the flake was found. The fix is recorded as **D233**, which states 50/50 runs per locale. That number is reported, not re-run here. The remaining acceptance for MW1 is the 20 local runs and 5 green main runs.
4. **QA-M16-4.** P06 row 11 re-verified the `0496478` fix on a `b6f5e1a` export: 11/11 pass, and removing the fix fails 4/4 and 2/7. P10 row 4 and P10-OW-10 predate that and say "not re-verified". The fix stands. What remains is P10-OW-10's browser timezone case and P06-OW-3r's intake residual.
5. **Mail queued at deletion.** The same mechanism is classified differently in three sections:
   - P09 row 4: `already_fixed`, with a race that was not reproduced (P09 R4 saw 0 POSTs).
   - P01 row 8(c): the race was not reproduced (R4c).
   - P02: `still_present`, because the P02 writer's repro R2 produced 1 POST after one workpool hop.

   These results agree. Nothing cancels the component send, so deletion is a race whose outcome depends on scheduling, and opt-out gives 1 POST every time. The backlog carries this as one item, P02-OW-3 (+P01-2, +P09-F8).
6. **Worktree hygiene.** At assembly time the worktree held 11 untracked throwaway files that no section writer claims:
   - `convex/zz_paudit_P09_1..5.test.ts`
   - `convex/zz_paudit_p06s_1.test.ts`
   - `convex/zz_paudit_p10k_1.test.ts`
   - `src/zz_paudit_p06s_sc.test.tsx` and `src/zz_paudit_p06s_ui.test.tsx`
   - `src/zz_paudit_p10k_2.test.tsx` and `src/zz_paudit_p10k_3.test.tsx`

   They are dated 08:05–08:17 IST, before any section file was written, and are probably left over from the P-audit run interrupted by the usage limit (D231). The assembler did not create or change them. Their owners, or the lead, need to remove them before the worktree is clean.


## P01

**P01: identity, consent, recovery. Primary class: still_present** (1 MEDIUM defect, 1 MEDIUM item blocked on live mail, several LOW items).

- **Base:** `main` at `a53a2bf`, worktree `recoup-wt-paudit`, 2026-09-23.
- **How this was produced:** an independent auditor pass, then a skeptic pass, both `claude-opus-5-5` (self-reported). The skeptic re-ran every auditor repro (10/10 pass, asserting current behaviour) and ran mutation tests in a throwaway copy of the worktree. All statuses below include the skeptic's corrections.
- **What the section writer checked:** re-ran the proving files at this HEAD, 7 files, 115/115 pass:
  - `npx vitest run convex/auth.test.ts convex/authFlow.test.ts convex/lib/authMail.test.ts convex/alerts.test.ts convex/alerts.flow.test.ts convex/http.test.ts convex/notify.fault.test.ts`
  - Also spot-checked every cited test line and code line. One correction: the unsubscribe reply text is at `http.ts:120`, not `:117`.
- **Changes from the M03 security baseline:** `2026-09-23-security-baseline.md` §1 marked these rows `already_fixed`. This pass reclassifies them as follows:
  - **Non-enumerating:** MEDIUM.
  - **Opt-out/unsubscribe:** LOW.
  - **Accurate suppression reason:** LOW (UI).
  - **Deleted accounts:** LOW.
  - **Opt-out while queued:** LOW.
  - **Bounded resend:** global-cap test gap.
- **Mutation IDs:** labels like M-A2, M-B and M-D refer to the skeptic's mutations. Each one removes or weakens the fix in a throwaway copy, and the entry records whether the cited tests fail.

### Sub-requirements

| # | Requirement | Status (after skeptic) | Evidence | Proving test |

| 1 | Expiring single-use verification codes | **already_fixed** | `lib/authMail.ts:197-240`: `Email({maxAge: VERIFICATION_CODE_TTL_S})`, re-spread at `:239`. TTL is 900 s (`limits.ts`). 8-digit CSPRNG code (`lib/authMail.ts:54-61`, `:200`). The library stores a sha256 hash, deletes the code on use, and keeps one code per account. `Email()`'s default `authorize` binds the code to the account (F1). **Skeptic's correction:** without `maxAge`, the fallback is `Email()`'s hard-coded 1 h, not 24 h. Mutation M-A2 (maxAge removed in both places) fails the TTL tests. M-A1 (removed from `Email()` only) is not a regression, because the re-spread keeps 900 s. | `auth.test.ts:118` (expired after TTL+1 s) and `lib/authMail.test.ts:34` fail under M-A2. `auth.test.ts:101` (replay). `authFlow.test.ts:324`, `:354` (cross-account binding) fail under M-B. All pass. |
| 2a | Bounded resend: per address | **already_fixed** | `lib/rateLimits.ts:15-39`. `authMailPerEmail` (3/h) is consumed inside `sendVerificationRequest` (`lib/authMail.ts:226`), so it covers every flow that sends a code. The reset phantom consume is at `auth.ts:299`. **Residual by design (D65):** no daily cap, so up to 72 codes a day can be sent to a known address. | Mutation M-C (drop `:226`) fails `auth.test.ts:258`, `:288`, `authFlow.test.ts:222`, `:526`, and `lib/authMail.test.ts:87`, `:101`. All pass. |

The call is then refused by the **signUp-only** pre-check at `auth.ts:260-263`, not by the consume. With the consume gone, reset and code mail have no deployment-wide cap. | None. See P01-8. |
| 3 | Non-enumerating responses | **still_present (MEDIUM)** | The public `auth:signIn` accepts the two code flows **with no code**. The library then mails a fresh code, because `implementation/signIn.ts:108` branches on `params.code !== undefined`. This creates two oracles:<br>**(a) email-verification, no code:** a known address resolves `{tokens:null}` and a code is mailed. The signUp `ACCOUNT_EXISTS` difference is a recorded decision, not open work (see Refuted). | None on main. `authFlow.test.ts:446` (F3) covers only requests that include a code. ### Remaining work

**P01-1: code flows accept requests with no code** (auditor P01-1 merged with skeptic SK-1)

- **Severity:** MEDIUM
- **Owner:** backend (M20/M29/M2C), security to review
- **Externally blocked:** No
- **Description:**
  - `flow:"email-verification"` and `flow:"reset-verification"` with no `code` reach the library, which issues and mails a new code (`implementation/signIn.ts:108`, `Password.ts:191`).
  - This one path is behind four problems: the email-verification enumeration oracle (S1), the reset-verification rate-limit oracle (S2), codes mailed to tombstoned accounts (R3/S3), and stray `users`, `authAccounts` and code rows that outlive `purgeAuth` (S2b).
  - The client always sends a string code (`SignIn.tsx:193`, `:278-281`). Resend goes through `signIn` or `reset`.
- **Smallest fix:** in `guardedAuthorize` (`convex/auth.ts`), right after the `authAttempt` limit (`:220`), add:

  ```ts
  if ((flow === "email-verification" || flow === "reset-verification") &&
      (typeof params.code !== "string" || params.code.length === 0))
    throw new ConvexError(INVALID_CODE_MESSAGE);
  ```

  Legitimate ways to request a code stay `signIn` with the password (D66) and `reset`.
- **Acceptance test:** add to `authFlow.test.ts`. Send no-code `{flow:"email-verification", email}` and `{flow:"reset-verification", email, newPassword}` against four addresses: known verified, known unverified, unknown, and tombstoned (`accountState` `deleting`). For every one:
  - the same `ConvexError(INVALID_CODE_MESSAGE)` `.data` is thrown;
  - `authMailTransport.send` is never called;
  - the `users` and `authAccounts` counts are unchanged;
  - the 4th call for a known address is **not** `RateLimited`.

  The S1/S2/S2b/S3 repros, inverted, must fail on current main. The existing F3 and D66 tests must pass unmodified.
- **Files:** `convex/auth.ts`, `convex/authFlow.test.ts`

**P01-2: opt-out, unsubscribe or deletion does not cancel a pending alert**

- **Severity:** LOW
- **Owner:** backend
- **Externally blocked:** No
- **Description:** an opt-out (`setAlerts(false)`) or a one-click unsubscribe that lands while the alert's component row is `pending` does not stop the provider POST (R4a/R4b). `requestDeletion`'s `suppressQueuedMail` relabels rows without cancelling them, and its comment (`account.ts:545`) says "the send already happened", which is not always true.
- **Smallest fix:** add a helper, `cancelPendingDrops(ctx, userId, reason)`:
  - Read the user's `mailLog` rows `by_user_status` `queued` (`.take(10)`).
  - For each row with an `outboundId` whose `agentmail.status(...).status === "pending"`, call `agentmail.cancel` inside try/catch.
  - If the cancel succeeds, mark the row `suppressed` with reason `opted_out` or `deleted`. Otherwise leave it `queued` so reconciliation records what actually happened.
  - Call the helper from `alerts.setAlerts({enabled:false})`, `alerts.unsubscribeByToken` and `account.suppressQueuedMail`, and correct the `:545` comment.
- **Acceptance test:** a real-component test in `notify.fault.test.ts` with a stubbed fetch:
  - Run `sendDrop`, then `setAlerts(false)` or `unsubscribeByToken` before the workpool runs, then drive the scheduler. Expect 0 POSTs and the row `suppressed/opted_out`.
  - Control case: when the POST completed before the opt-out, the row still ends `sent`.
  - Same for `requestDeletion`: 0 POSTs whichever of purge and workpool runs first.
- **Files:** `convex/alerts.ts`, `convex/account.ts`, `convex/notify.ts` (helper), `convex/notify.fault.test.ts`

**P01-3: Settings toggle shows ON while the address is suppressed**

- **Severity:** LOW
- **Owner:** frontend (M24/M2A)
- **Externally blocked:** No
- **Description:** after a bounce or complaint, the Settings toggle shows ON next to "Turn alerts back on to resume". The only click available sends `enabled:false` and leaves the suppression in place.
- **Smallest fix:** in `Settings.tsx:309`, draw the toggle as `checked={alerts.alertsEnabled && alerts.suppressedReason === null}`. One click then calls `setAlerts({enabled:true})`, which clears the suppression and rotates the token. The alternative is to have `alerts.settings` return an `effectiveEnabled` field.
- **Acceptance test:** new `src/pages/Settings.alerts.test.tsx` (happy-dom):
  - With `{alertsEnabled:true, suppressedReason:"bounced"}`, the "Price alerts" checkbox is unchecked, the notice is shown, and one click calls `setAlerts({enabled:true})`.
  - With `suppressedReason:null` and `alertsEnabled:true`, the box is checked.
- **Files:** `src/pages/Settings.tsx`, `src/pages/Settings.alerts.test.tsx` (new)

**P01-4: consent and unsubscribe copy contradicts behaviour**

- **Severity:** LOW
- **Owner:** ops/lead (decision), then frontend
- **Externally blocked:** No
- **Description:**
  - `SignIn.tsx:44` and `Settings.tsx:324` say alerts start "once you turn them on" or "opt in", but they are on by default after verification (`alerts.ts:32`, `:78`; `Privacy.tsx:130`). No DECISIONS entry records the default-on choice.
  - `Privacy.tsx:135` promises a one-click "Unsubscribe" link in every alert. The body has none, and the headers exist only when `CONVEX_SITE_URL` is https (R6/S5).
- **Smallest fix:**
  - The lead records whether alerts are default-on or explicit opt-in in DECISIONS.
  - If default-on: one shared constant in `convex/lib/privacyFacts.ts` ("Price alerts are on by default for your verified address; turn them off anytime in Settings") replaces the SignIn and Settings strings.
  - Then either reword `Privacy.tsx:135` to "supports one-click unsubscribe in mail clients that show it", or add a footer line to `notify.buildMessage` with the https unsubscribe URL or the Settings link.
- **Acceptance test:** a copy test asserting that SignIn, Settings and Privacy render the shared constant. If a footer is added, `notify.test.ts` asserts the body contains the unsubscribe URL when `CONVEX_SITE_URL` is https.
- **Files:** `src/pages/SignIn.tsx`, `src/pages/Settings.tsx`, `src/pages/Privacy.tsx`, `convex/lib/privacyFacts.ts`; optionally `convex/notify.ts` and `convex/notify.test.ts`; `docs/team/DECISIONS.md`

- **Smallest fix:**

  - **(b)** Add R7 as a test: sign up, verify, create a second session, run reset then reset-verification. Assert that no earlier `authSessions` id survives and that `signIn` with the old password throws `WRONG_CREDENTIALS`.

- **Severity:** LOW
- **Owner:** qa

It throws `RateLimited` with name `authMailGlobal`, and `send` is not called. Because `reset` has no global pre-check, the test fails with `:227` removed.

**P01-9: a stale unsubscribe token gets a false success reply** (skeptic SK-3)

- **Severity:** LOW
- **Owner:** backend, security to review
- **Externally blocked:** No
- **Description:** a POST to `/alerts/unsubscribe` with a rotated or stale token answers "You will no longer receive price alert emails from Recoup." while alerts stay on (`http.ts:120`, `alerts.ts:100-106`). After a re-enable, unsubscribing from any older alert fails silently while reporting success.
- **Smallest fix:** make the `http.ts:120` text truthful and still non-enumerating, for example "If this link is current, price alerts are now off. You can check or change this in Recoup Settings." The alternative, honouring earlier tokens for disabling only, reverses F11a and needs a lead decision.
- **Acceptance test:** in `http.test.ts`, run `setAlerts(false)`, then `setAlerts(true)`, then POST with the old token. The response does not claim the user is unsubscribed, `settings.alertsEnabled` is still true, and the valid-token and garbage-token responses are byte-identical.
- **Files:** `convex/http.ts`, `convex/http.test.ts`

**P01-10: no test pins the CSPRNG** (skeptic SK-4)

- **Severity:** LOW
- **Owner:** qa, security
- **Externally blocked:** No
- **Description:** no test would catch the verification codes or the unsubscribe token moving off the CSPRNG. M-K passes 66/66 and M-L passes 83/83.
- **Smallest fix:** add a spy-based test.
- **Acceptance test:** `vi.spyOn(globalThis.crypto,"getRandomValues")` is called when `authMail("verify").generateVerificationToken()` runs and when `setAlerts(true)` creates or rotates a token. The test fails under the `Math.random` mutation.
- **Files:** `convex/lib/authMail.test.ts`, `convex/alerts.test.ts`

**P01-11: expired codes pile up and can collide** (skeptic SK-5)

- **Severity:** LOW
- **Owner:** backend
- **Externally blocked:** No
- **Description:** the D65 8-digit code, combined with the library's `.unique()` lookup by hash, means two accounts holding the same code both fail verification with the generic error (S7). Expired codes of verified accounts are never deleted, so the chance of a collision grows over time.
- **Smallest fix:** add a bounded retention step, using the existing retention page size, that deletes `authVerificationCodes` rows with `expirationTime < now`, so the live set stays roughly the 15-minute window. A longer code needs a D65 amendment by the lead, so it is not part of the smallest fix.
- **Acceptance test:** a retention test shows the sweep deletes an expired code belonging to a verified account and leaves an unexpired one. With `generateRandomString` mocked to a fixed value, two accounts whose codes were issued more than one TTL apart can each verify with their own current code.
- **Files:** `convex/retention.ts`, `convex/retention.test.ts`

### Refuted

- **Auditor P01-6 (signUp `ACCOUNT_EXISTS` oracle, "never recorded as accepted"):** refuted as open work.
  - **Recorded decision:** D94 F2 routes "signUp on an existing account … map to ACCOUNT_EXISTS regardless of secret", and D91 publishes `ACCOUNT_EXISTS_MESSAGE` as the client contract.
  - **Rate-bounded:** `authSignUpGlobal` (200/h) is consumed before the existence probe (`auth.ts:258`), and `authSignUp` allows 20/h per address.
  - **Takeaway:** it is at most a documentation note. The unbounded oracle is the no-code verification path (P01-1).

### Notes

- **Auditor evidence corrections applied:**
  - The `maxAge` fallback is 1 h, not 24 h (row 1).
  - `testing.ts:134` writes `emailVerificationTime`, not `users.email` (row 4).
  - The unsubscribe reply is at `http.ts:120`.
- **Numbering:** IDs P01-8 to P01-11 are the skeptic's SK-2 to SK-5. SK-1 is merged into P01-1. P01-6 is refuted, so the numbering has a gap.
- **Worktree:** the only file this section writer created is this file. Untracked `zz_paudit_*` files from other lanes (P06, P09, P10) were present in the worktree and were not touched.

## P02

**Outbound reliability (claim emails and price-drop alerts).** Audited on main `a53a2bf` from the `recoup-wt-paudit` worktree. An auditor and a skeptic worked independently; this section applies the skeptic's corrections.

**Overall: `still_present`.** The original P02 rows and both parts of S-M03-1 are fixed, and tests prove the fixes:

- **At-most-once POST.** `convex/mail.ts:20` sets `retryAttempts: 1`.
- **Ambiguous outcomes are `unknown`, never failures.** See `convex/drafts.ts:73-86`, `drafts.ts:1405-1410` and `convex/notify.ts:528-536`.
- **No blind resend.** Sending again goes through `resendAfterUnknown` (`drafts.ts:1232-1290`).

Four medium gaps remain:

- Opting out or deleting the account does not cancel mail that is already queued.
- Claim emails have no sweep and no recheck the user can reach.
- A resend does not cancel an earlier attempt that is still pending.
- "Sending…" shows forever in three places.

Live provider behaviour is `externally_blocked` (D83 placeholder keys; production is unauthorized, D136). Every local mechanism was exercised through the real AgentMail component and workpool in convex-test, with `fetch` stubbed at the provider boundary. No real provider was called.

**How the evidence was checked:**

- **Skeptic pass.** It completed. It changed one classification (the indefinite "Sending" clause) and refuted nothing. It added four items; two of them duplicate auditor items and are merged below.
- **Writer re-run.** The P02 proving files passed:
  - `npx vitest run convex/drafts.delivery.test.ts convex/notify.delivery.test.ts convex/notify.fault.test.ts convex/mailEvents.test.ts src/lib/delivery.test.ts src/lib/drops.test.ts`: 6 files, 64/64.
  - `npx vitest run convex/drafts.prepare.test.ts -t DA-B-11 src/components/claim/Composer.test.tsx`: 9/9.
- **Writer throwaway.** `convex/zz_paudit_P02_w1.test.ts` (3/3 PASS, each asserting current behaviour; deleted afterwards) reproduced three defects independently:
  - P02-SK-1: 8 days after a confirmed send, `sendStatus` is `null` although `draft.agentmailMessageId` is `mid-1`.
  - P02-OW-1 / R4: a resend while the earlier attempt is still pending produces 2 POSTs.
  - P02-OW-3 / R2: `requestDeletion` after one workpool hop leaves the component row `pending`, and 1 POST follows.

### Sub-requirements

| Requirement | Status (after skeptic) | Evidence | Proving test |

PASS. **Skeptic caveat:** no convex-test test would fail if the enqueue and the local write were split into two mutations. |

### Refuted

None. The skeptic refuted none of the auditor's open-work items or classifications.

Two skeptic findings were merged rather than listed separately, because they duplicate auditor items:

- **P02-SK-2 → P02-OW-4:** dashboard "Sending…" for an unknown send.
- **P02-SK-4 → P02-OW-5:** unbounded polling and no pruning of `unknown` alerts. The skeptic's added detail, that the backoff restarts after the 7-day component cleanup, is folded into OW-5.

## P03

**ShopSavvy market lookups: state machine, bounded spend, recovery, migration**

**Overall: still_present, in a narrower form than the 2026-09-21 baseline.** QA-1 (M1A, D149) is fixed. The seven-state server machine exists. The transactional claim, retry cap and daily budgets all hold, and tests would fail if any of them were removed. What remains:

- No user can recover a settled failure, because no UI calls `api.market.refresh`.
- A key problem (removed, expired or out of credit) either wipes stored state or blocks watches for good.
- History windows can still be bought twice.
- `migrateStamps` ignores legacy "no history" evidence and cannot be re-run.

**Where this was checked.** Commit a53a2bf, worktree `recoup-wt-paudit`. `origin/main` 364ac39 has no diff to `market.ts`, `limits.ts` or `lib/shopsavvy.ts`, and still has no UI caller of `market.refresh`.

**How it was checked.**

- **Committed tests.** The section writer re-ran them: `npx vitest run convex/market.test.ts convex/marketFlow.test.ts convex/lifecycle.test.ts convex/lib/shopsavvy.test.ts` → 4 files, 112/112 passed. The writer also re-read every cited line at a53a2bf.
- **Repros.** Repros R1–R6, V1, V2, O1, A1, A2 and S3/S6 are throwaway tests from the auditor and the skeptic. Each "defect confirmed" repro was reproduced independently by both passes. All throwaway files have been deleted.
- **Mutation run (skeptic).** 29 mutants of `market.ts` were run against market, marketFlow and lifecycle tests (67 tests). An unchanged copy passed 67/67. 23 mutants were killed. 6 survived: `classify_4xx_retry`, `success_auto_open`, `record_notrunning`, `nokey_stamp_record`, `migrate_no_resched`, `migrate_no_cursor`.

### Refuted

- **P03-D sub-item (a): a manual refresh of `empty_result` re-buys the identical window at once (auditor R4).** This is accepted scope, not a defect. D96 (DECISIONS.md:126) allows a manual refresh to retry `empty_result` or `terminal_failure` immediately, bounded by the per-user budget of 5 per day. `market.test.ts:395` pins it. The proposed "refuse same-day refresh" fix is dropped. The other re-purchase paths remain open (P03-B, P03-D, P03-E).
- **P03-F's claim that "RUNBOOK §3 tells operators to re-run both migrations with `restart:true`".** This is false. `restart:true` appears only for `normalizeLegacyAccounts` (RUNBOOK.md:118-125; RELEASE.md:84-87). The real gap is that the cursor stays parked after a completed run and there is no restart arg. That is now P03-SK-4.
- **Evidence claim that "`market.test.ts:488` pins 401/403 as terminal" (auditor, rows 2 and 5).** This is wrong: the mutant `classify_4xx_retry` survives all 67 tests. The row statuses are unaffected (row 2 stays already_fixed on its other evidence, and row 5 stays still_present), but the retryable-vs-terminal split for 4xx is recorded as unproven (P03-SK-3).

## P04

**Scope.** Checks at the provider boundary (ShopSavvy): product identity, condition, quantity, currency, shipping, timestamps, staleness, money, outliers, source labelling, missing credentials, and whether provider data can ever create a claim or alert.

**Overall: still_present.** Audited on main `a53a2bf`. The upstream commits up to `origin/main 1ba15d8` do not touch P04 logic; they add only the purchasedAt clamp in watches.ts.

**Basis.**
- **Auditor:** checked every row, with throwaway repros and mutation proofs.
- **Skeptic:** reproduced every still_present item. It corrected two rows the auditor had marked already_fixed and filled in the rows that were cut off in the auditor's report.
- **This write-up:** re-ran the proving files: `npx vitest run convex/lib/shopsavvy.test.ts convex/market.test.ts convex/offers.test.ts convex/lib/verdict.test.ts convex/marketFlow.test.ts convex/watches.test.ts` gave **6 files, 254/254 passed**. It spot-read every cited line. A throwaway, `convex/zz_paudit_P04w_1.test.ts` (2/2 passed, deleted), confirmed two skeptic claims:
  - 8 of 8 unlisted non-new condition strings survive `flattenHistory`.
  - Four market points from 2011 still produce `wait … 2011`.

### Refuted

- **No open-work item was refuted.** The skeptic reproduced every still_present item with its own throwaways (R1–R7, S1–S8, U1–U3, all deleted).
- **Part of OW2's fix was refuted.** The auditor suggested comparing `snapshot.title` with `watch.name` using `titleSimilarity`. The skeptic showed this cannot work: ShopSavvy is queried by the watched URL itself (`market.ts:138`), so the snapshot title is always the watched product. The mismatched variant is a per-offer row, and the fixture has no per-offer title. The step is removed from OW2's smallest fix.
- **Two already_fixed rows were refuted as overstated:**
  - Row 4 (condition in history), now still_present.
  - Row 11 (timestamps), now changed_scope_with_evidence, with 11b still_present.

### Worktree hygiene (this write-up)

The only file created was the throwaway `convex/zz_paudit_P04w_1.test.ts`, deleted after its run. No P04 files remain in `git status`. The other untracked `zz_paudit_*` files belong to concurrent auditors (P09, p06s, p10k) and were not touched. No production file was edited, and nothing was committed, stashed or pushed.

## P05

**P05: bounded reads, then filtering. Archived, candidate and converted rows must not hide or double-count live ones, and sampled data must never be shown as complete.**

**Overall: still_present.** Audited on `a53a2bf` (worktree `paudit`). The original baseline fixes still hold: archived watches and purchases no longer hide active ones, candidate offers no longer hide confirmed ones, converted watches are counted once, and the QA-2 charts keep currencies apart. Each of these has a passing test. The same defect has come back in Mission-2 code:
- `recovery.summary` reads rows oldest-first and filters them only after the bounded read.
- Opportunities on archived transactions stay `open`.
- The Recovery-by-currency panel has no "partial" label.

Other gaps of the same kind:
- the QA-7 30-watch cap;
- the Price Drop Alerts tile counts one emailed drop twice;
- offer prices are compared and labelled across currencies;
- the Board banner cuts older needs_review purchases;
- dismissing a partly credited claim erases its confirmed money.

Nothing here needs a real provider, so nothing is externally blocked.

**Verification.** The auditor and the skeptic each reproduced every still_present row with their own throwaway tests. For this section I also:
- re-ran the proving test files: `npx vitest run convex/insights.test.ts convex/dashboard.test.ts convex/tracking.test.ts convex/recovery.test.ts convex/ledgerFixtures.test.ts convex/purchases.test.ts src/components/dashboard/StatCards.test.tsx src/components/dashboard/model.test.ts` gave **8 files, 166 passed**;
- reproduced R2 and R7 a third time with throwaway `convex/zz_paudit_p05w_1.test.ts` (W1, W2: 2 passed), then deleted it;
- re-read every cited file:line on `a53a2bf`.

The skeptic pass completed, and its corrections are applied below.

### Refuted

None. The skeptic re-ran every proving test the auditor cited (7 files, 133 passed) and reproduced every still_present item independently. It refuted none of P05-OW1…OW9. It changed four things instead:
- It widened OW1: the bound is reached in normal use.
- It widened OW4: bought watches take up the 30 slots.
- It widened OW6: the server also compares currencies when the watch's currency is null.
- It downgraded two parts of the auditor's already_fixed rows: the UI labels have no regression test (row 9), and the candidate-offer test only guards against the original ascending read (row 5, P05-SK-5).

### Worktree

This section's throwaway `convex/zz_paudit_p05w_1.test.ts` was deleted, and so were the auditor's and skeptic's P05 files. No tracked file was changed, and `git status` shows no P05 file other than this section. Untracked `zz_paudit_*` files from other auditors (P06s, P09, P10k) are still present; they were left untouched.

## P06

**Time and freshness: reactive clocks, freshness fields, window expiry, server-clock re-checks**

**Overall: still_present, in a narrower form than the 2026-09-21 baseline.** Three things are fixed and guarded: QA-3/DA-B-1 (`approveAndSend` re-checks the window on the server clock), the separate freshness fields on watches, and QA-M16-4 (fixed on main by `0496478`, D219, after the audited revision). The remaining gaps:

- **Stored R01 evaluations outlive their window.** After the user deadline passes, the card, the new `/opportunities` row and the dashboard "Potential" tile still show eligible money and "Start a claim". Nothing re-evaluates the opportunity until the user clicks `openCase`, and the server does refuse that click.
- **QA-4.** Owned items have no data-age signal, so a 20-day-old drop reads "Claim now".
- **Weak clock guards.** Seven coarse-`now` queries can be switched to the wall clock without failing any committed test. The only real guards are two literal-string greps.
- **Untested clock mechanisms.** The open-tab clock hooks have no tests, and neither does `openCase`'s server-clock re-evaluation.
- **Small items:** an unbounded `now` on `recovery.summary`, wall-clock reads in four internal queries, an unbounded "Checking…" state in ItemTracker, and routes that crash on a device clock skewed by more than 24 h.

Nothing in P06 needs a provider or production. Every mechanism was verified locally.

**Where this was checked.**

- **Audited revision:** commit a53a2bf in worktree `recoup-wt-paudit`, used by both the auditor and the skeptic.
- **Re-verified by the section writer:** the rows that changed after the audit, against `origin/main` b6f5e1a. Local `main` is 0c7cbb7 and contains `0496478` too.
- **Method:** `origin/main` was exported with `git archive` into the session scratchpad, with `node_modules` symlinked. No repo checkout was touched.

| File | Change between a53a2bf and b6f5e1a |
|---|---|
| `convex/watches.ts` | Adds `purchasedAtNotAfterNow` at :68. Lines shift by +12: `assertCoarseNow` :71 becomes :83, `list` :344 becomes :356. |
| `convex/purchases.ts` | Adds the clamp calls. |
| `convex/recovery.ts` | D222/D223 claim-split changes only. The deadline and money-tile logic is unchanged; lines shift by +10 (`summary` :352 becomes :362, :429/:431 become :439/:441). |
| `convex/drafts.ts` | Lines shift by +5 (:790 becomes :795, :851 becomes :856). |
| `src/lib/ui.ts` | New `fromDateInput`/`toDateInput`. |
| `src/components/opportunity/OpportunityRow.tsx` | New (M24, 3c46cc4). |
| `convex/crons.ts`, `convex/opportunities.ts`, `convex/tracking.ts`, `src/lib/priceStats.ts`, `src/components/purchase/ItemTracker.tsx` | No change. |

**How it was checked.**

- **Committed tests re-run by the writer at a53a2bf:** `npx vitest run convex/drafts.prepare.test.ts convex/watches.test.ts convex/purchases.test.ts convex/freshness.test.ts convex/priceWatch.test.ts convex/offers.test.ts` gave 6 files, 242/242 passed.
- **Committed tests re-run by the writer on the b6f5e1a export:**
  - `convex/purchases.date.test.ts src/lib/purchaseDate.test.tsx`: 11/11 passed.
  - `convex/drafts.prepare.test.ts convex/watches.test.ts convex/purchases.test.ts convex/recovery.test.ts src/pages/Opportunities.test.tsx`: 204/204 passed.
- **Removal checks by the writer on the b6f5e1a export.** Each was an on-disk edit to the scratch copy, restored afterwards and diffed against `origin/main`.
  - (1) `purchasedAtNotAfterNow` without the `Math.min(…, now)`: `purchases.date.test.ts` fails 4 of 4.
  - (2) `fromDateInput` returning noon for today: `purchaseDate.test.tsx` fails 2 of 7.
  - (3) `watches.list` with `const now = Date.now()`: only the grep `watches.test.ts:1061` fails (1 of 85).
  - (4) `watches.list` with `const now = new Date().getTime()`: 85/85 pass, so the grep is evaded.
  - (5) `tracking.ts:162` switched to `Date.now()`: 138/138 pass (tracking, freshness, insights, budget, recovery, dashboard, readBudget).
  - (6) `recovery.ts:367` switched to `Date.now()`: 138/138 pass.
- **The auditor's saved repros** (`zz_paudit_p06_1`, `_2`, `_ui`, copied from the scratchpad into the export only) were re-run on b6f5e1a:
  - 14 passed. The OW-1, QA-4, stale-card, clock-hook and unbounded-`now` defects are still present on main.
  - The 3 QA-M16-4 defect repros now fail, which means the defect is gone: `purchases.create` stores +0 instead of +4 h, and `fromDateInput(today)` returns `now`.
- **Mutation runs by the auditor and the skeptic** at a53a2bf used in-memory vitest transforms, so source-grep tests that read the file from disk could not see them. Those runs are cited per row.
- **Throwaway files.** The auditor and the skeptic deleted theirs. The writer created none in the repo; this section file is the only new file.

### Refuted

- **P06-OW-3 (auditor): "QA-M16-4 still present; fix in progress on branch m15d, no commits yet".** Closed; no longer open work. The fix is on local `main` (0c7cbb7) and `origin/main` (b6f5e1a) as `0496478`, accepted in D219. The writer verified it:
  - The fix tests pass: 11/11.
  - Removing the server clamp fails 4/4 tests, and reverting the client's today→now mapping fails 2/7.
  - The auditor's three defect repros fail on main, so the defect is gone.

  Only the D219 intake residual remains (P06-OW-3r).
- **OW-1's original fix (a), "filter the money tiles by the client's `now`", as the primary fix.** Dropped (skeptic). It contradicts D73, and the `now` argument is unbounded (OW-7). The authoritative fix is the scheduled re-evaluation (OW-1 (1)); the display changes are interim only.
- **Evidence claim "`watches.test.ts:1086` and `offers.test.ts:668`/`:681` fail if a `Date.now()` is added back" (auditor, row 1).** Wrong. Those tests advance the system clock together with `now`, and `Date.now()` mutations in `watches.list`, `watches.get` and `offers.listForWatch` survive 85/85 and 40/40 (skeptic). Row 1 stays already_fixed on the greps alone. Row 2 was split out as changed_scope_with_evidence.
- **Evidence claim "a dynamic S vs S+20h check proves no public query depends on the wall clock" (auditor).** Weaker than stated. Its fixtures had nothing time-sensitive, so it missed the tracking and insights mutations (skeptic). It is kept as supporting evidence only.
- **"No committed src test uses fake timers" (auditor, row 7).** Out of date on main: `src/lib/purchaseDate.test.tsx` uses `vi.useFakeTimers` and `setSystemTime`. It never advances timers or ticks a hook, though, so row 7 and OW-6 stand.
- The skeptic refuted no auditor open-work item outright. OW-2 through OW-8 were truncated in the auditor's report the skeptic saw. The section writer re-checked OW-3 (closed above) and OW-7 (still reproduces on b6f5e1a) directly.

## P07

**P07: performance and job hardening. It covers read counts and read bytes, response size, nested reads, fan-out, invalidation, job throughput, per-user fairness, failing closed when a provider budget runs out, and retention with bounded deletion.**

**Overall: still_present.** Audited on `a53a2bf` (worktree `paudit`). The auditor and the skeptic both classify the item as still_present.

**What holds, each with a test that would fail if the fix were removed:**
- Document and index-range budgets for dashboards, cron fan-outs and the Mission-2 read models.
- Fan-out and per-user caps.
- Invalidation: no wall clock in queries, and no spurious invalidation of approvals.
- Provider budgets fail closed.
- Most retention steps.
- Financial history is preserved.
- Account purge is bounded and resumable.

**What is open.** Three Mission-1/2 paths read unbounded bytes or starve rows:
- The hourly `intake.retryFailed` cron overflows the 16 MiB read limit, and when it does it stops inbound retries for every user.
- Its budget-paused pass scans a window that ordinary mail fills, so older paused rows are never retried.
- `claims.get` ships every raw inbound message of the thread to the client, and nothing uses them.

Two retention gaps contradict the Privacy page:
- `needs_review` payloads are never cleared.
- Inbound copies held in the shared alerts inbox are never cleared.

`marketPrices` has no retention, and its readers use the oldest 120 points. Several LOW retention, throughput and measurement items remain.

All open work is local except the live provider and platform rehearsal. That rehearsal is externally blocked: D83 (the dev keys are placeholders) and D136 (production is not authorized).

**Verification.**
- **Auditor:** reproduced every still_present row with a throwaway `convex/zz_paudit_p07_1.test.ts` (11 tests, since deleted).
- **Skeptic:** re-ran the proving tests in 21 files (450 passed, 1 expected fail). It reproduced the rows independently with four throwaway `zz_paudit_p07s_*` files, all deleted.
- **For this section:**
  - re-ran `npx vitest run convex/retention.test.ts convex/fairness.test.ts convex/lib/privacyFacts.test.ts convex/readBudget.test.ts convex/opportunities.budget.test.ts`: **5 files, 63 passed + 1 expected fail** (`fairness.test.ts:220`, F-D74-1).
  - re-read every cited file:line on `a53a2bf`.

The skeptic pass completed, and its corrections are applied below.

### Refuted

- **P07-W9: retention chains have no in-flight guard (row 8b).** It reproduces: two `retention.sweep` calls leave two pending continuations. The skeptic showed it is harmless. Both chains read and write the same opsState cursor inside serializable mutations, so they share pages and never duplicate or corrupt work. When one chain finishes the last step, it stops. The other finds `{step:0, page:null}`, runs at most one extra full cycle, and stops. That is a bounded redundant cycle, not a throughput or correctness defect. No fix is needed.
- **Row 8c: the D75 cycle rescans whole tables daily.** This is the design, not a defect. Every call reads a bounded page (25 or 200 rows) and resumes from a persisted cursor, and a daily cycle finishes far inside 24 h at the current scale. The real root cause, `processedEvents` rows that are never deleted, is tracked as P07-W6 (merged with P07-SK-5).

Not refuted, but corrected. The skeptic moved two rows to still_present:
- **Row 9b**, per-user fairness: the paused-retry window starves older paused rows.
- **Row 15b**, AgentMail copies: shared alerts-inbox copies are never retained or purged.

The skeptic also made three evidence corrections:
- `claims.get` overflows at 40–45 replies of 200 KB, not exactly 40.
- `needsAttention` needs multibyte text plus failed rows; ASCII rows cannot overflow it.
- Byte-aware paging also exists in account export/purge, `sweepRecovery` and the orphan sweep.

This file is the only P07 output, and it is not committed. Untracked `zz_paudit_*` files from other auditors (P09, p06s, p10k) are still present; they were left untouched.

### Remaining work

Ordered by severity. Items the skeptic added or reshaped carry its SK id.

**P08-W1 · medium · ingestion-integrations (M23) with security review · not externally blocked**
- **Description:** a PAN detected in an uploaded PDF is discarded when the upload has no X-Doc-Type. declareDocType then re-opens extraction (not_requested) once `live_document_extraction` is on, so a document with a full card number could reach the extraction model. This breaks DA-A-8/SEC-SD-2 and declareDocType's documented invariant. It is latent today (no consumer, and the D145 flag is off), but it **must land before M23's extractor is wired or the flag is enabled**. It is unchanged on origin/main b6f5e1a.
- **Smallest fix:** in `uploadExtractionStatus`, move `if (input.panDetected) return { status: "store_only", summary: STATUS_SUMMARY.pan, … }` above the awaiting_doc_type branch (after the HEIC and encrypted checks). The PAN summary is then persisted, and `isStickyBlock` keeps it through any re-declaration. The alternative is to persist `panDetected` on the row and pass it from declareDocType.
- **Acceptance test:** a new `evidence.test.ts` case with the flag on. An upload of `%PDF… (Paid with 4111 1111 1111 1111)` with no X-Doc-Type → store_only with STATUS_SUMMARY.pan; declareDocType("receipt") → still store_only. The result is the same when the flag is switched on after upload. The existing DA-A-8 tests (`evidence.test.ts:363-447`) stay green.
- **Files:** `convex/evidence.ts`, `convex/evidence.test.ts`.

**P08-W2 (+ P08-SK-1) · medium · qa (M25) · not externally blocked**
- **Description:** the M16 isolation guard does not fail closed across the whole deployable surface.
  - **Fixed on origin/main 6a2a1fc (D223), not in the audited tree:** the soft it.todo (`isolationM1.test.ts:419` at a53a2bf) and the missing HTTP-route enumeration.
  - **Open in both trees:** the glob `["./*.ts", "!./*.*.ts"]` (a53a2bf `:68`, origin/main `:77`) loads only top-level modules. Convex already deploys function modules from `convex/lib/` (authMail, authMigrate), so a public function added under any subdirectory would be client-callable and unflagged.
- **Smallest fix:** once 6a2a1fc is in the target tree, keep its hard-fail and change the glob to `["./**/*.ts", "!./**/*.*.ts", "!./_generated/**"]`. Name functions by module path (`lib/authMail.recordE2ECode`, matching api paths) and keep the isPublic filter.
- **Acceptance test:** passing `uncoveredOf` a module map with a public `zzLeak.peek` query under key `"./lib/zzLeak.ts"` reports it as uncovered; an internalQuery at the same path is not reported. Adding any uncovered public function or HTTP route makes `isolationM1.test.ts` fail. The file is green at the target main.
- **Files:** `convex/isolationM1.test.ts`.

**P08-W4 (+ P08-SK-2) · medium (raised from low by the skeptic) · qa (M25) with security · not externally blocked**
- **Description:** there is no permanent reflective boundary test.
  - **Auth and tombstone:** gating for 19 of the 22 new public functions (all except transactions.list, facts.list and facts.answer, plus the HTTP routes), and validator presence for all 79, were proven only by this audit's deleted throwaway sweep.
  - **Validators:** a per-function swap of requireUserId for getAuthUserId, or a dropped `returns:`, would pass CI.
  - **Ledger amounts:** the new ledger mutations have no permanent NaN/Infinity/negative/fraction/1e15 test.
- **Smallest fix:** commit a recursive reflective sweep (e.g. `convex/boundary.reflective.test.ts`):
  - generate args from `exportArgs()`;
  - call each function signed out and as a tombstoned user;
  - assert the exact outcome from a per-function expectation map ("throws Not signed in", "throws account deleted", or "returns the declared empty value" for the 10 getAuthUserId-pattern queries), with 0 app-table writes and 0 fetches (fetch stubbed, tables snapshotted);
  - assert `exportReturns()` for every non-`auth.*` function.

  Add the p08_4 malformed-amount matrix for recordProvisionalCredit, finalizeProvisionalCredit, reverseProvisionalCredit and recordNonCashRemedy to `boundary.test.ts`.
- **Acceptance test:** all functions pass at the target main. Each of these makes the sweep fail and name the function:
  - replacing requireUserId with getAuthUserId in opportunities.openCase;
  - the same in recovery.summary (the map expects a throw, not an empty value);
  - removing `returns:` from evidence.setPinned.
- **Files:** `convex/boundary.reflective.test.ts` (new), `convex/boundary.test.ts`.

**P08-SK-3 · low · backend (M20/M29/M2C) · not externally blocked**
- **Description:** `purchases.confirm` re-evaluates up to 50 items in the same mutation (D180) with no rate limiter, so a scripted client bypasses the M03 §3.7 `evaluate` read-amplification bucket that openCase and reevaluate consume. In the repro, 80 evaluating confirms in one minute were all accepted and wrote 4,000 evaluation rows. The effect is limited to the caller's own rows and operator cost. It is unchanged on origin/main b6f5e1a.
- **Smallest fix:** in `purchases.confirm`, only when an evaluation will run (`purchaseLevelChanged || changedItemSubjects.length > 0`), consume `rateLimiter.limit(ctx, "evaluate", { key: userId })` after the ownership and validation checks and before `evaluateTransaction`. If it is refused, either save the edit and skip the evaluation with a visible "will re-check later" state, or refuse the call.
- **Acceptance test:** the 61st evaluating confirm within a minute writes no evaluation rows (or is refused). A no-change confirm is never limited. The first 60 evaluating confirms still evaluate in the same call. `purchases`, `facts.reevaluate` and `opportunities` tests stay green.
- **Files:** `convex/purchases.ts`, `convex/purchases.test.ts` (or `facts.reevaluate.test.ts`).

**P08-W3 (+ P08-SK-4) · low · ingestion-integrations (M28), plus frontend (M24/M2A) for the confirm affordance · not externally blocked**
- **Description:** S-M03-6 is only half fixed. A merchant reply with senderMismatch true (including an address-less From) still writes promised_credit and moves the claim to `promised` (`replies.ts:261,302,304`). The amount reaches the recovery.summary Promised tile, and reminders follow a spoofed promise. D174/D178 say a spoofed promise must never do this. This is the replies half of DA-B-3 (M28, wave 2), still open at a53a2bf and on origin/main b6f5e1a.
- **Smallest fix:** in `replies.apply`, when senderMismatch is true, insert the reply with promisedCents and set `claim.attentionAt`, but skip applyEvent and the status patch. Add an owner-only one-shot `replies.confirmPromise({ replyId })`, modelled on `intake.confirmRefundEmail` (requireUserId → owned reply → one-shot → applyEvent with the same derived key).
- **Acceptance test:** a reply from `refunds@evil.example` (promise, 400.00) → 0 ledgerEvents, claim status unchanged, Promised tile 0, attentionAt set. After the owner confirms → exactly one promised_credit 40000. B confirming A's reply gets the identical not-found (a new isolationM1 probe). Same-domain replies behave as before (`replies.test.ts` unchanged).
- **Files:** `convex/replies.ts`, `convex/replies.sender.test.ts`, `convex/isolationM1.test.ts`, `src/components/claim/*`.

**P08-SK-5 · low · security · not externally blocked**
- **Description:** the SEC-AI-1 static guard asserts constant system arguments only for five hard-coded files (`priceWatch.prompt.test.ts:134`). An `extract()` call site in any other file is collected but never checked. The M23 document-extraction caller is expected next.
- **Smallest fix:** replace the `it.each` over the file allowlist with one `it()` that loops over every collected `site`. Keep the "finds the known call sites" test.
- **Acceptance test:** adding ``extract("x", S, `${row.name}`, u)`` to any `convex/**/*.ts` outside the five files makes the test fail. All current sites still pass.
- **Files:** `convex/priceWatch.prompt.test.ts`.

**P08-W6 · low · backend (M20/M29/M2C) · not externally blocked (the live effect is D83-blocked)**
- **Description:** carried S-M03-7 / D133 N4. Three direct provider fetches have no timeout: `profiles.ts:296` createInboxRemote, `account.ts:1000` deleteInbox and `lib/authMail.ts:176` sign-in code send. A hung provider holds the action until the platform limit. This is unchanged on origin/main b6f5e1a.
- **Smallest fix:** add `signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10_000)` to each fetch, as `market.ts:143` does, and map the abort to the existing sanitized error paths.
- **Acceptance test:** with fetch stubbed to never resolve, each call rejects within the timeout with its existing owner-safe message:
  - ensureInbox releases its provisioning claim;
  - deleteInbox is retried by its schedule;
  - auth mail reports "Could not send the email right now".

  The profiles, account and authMail tests stay green.

**P08-W5 · low · security (inventory owner) with ops/lead · not externally blocked**
- **Description:** `docs/reviews/endpoint-inventory.md` is stale: 57 functions at c78c338, no HTTP route table, and old line numbers. a53a2bf has 79 app functions, 3 framework exports and 10 routes; origin/main b6f5e1a has 83 app functions. Its counting command (`convex/*.ts`) and its claim that `convex/lib/**` exports no functions ignore subdirectory modules.

None. The skeptic refuted none of the auditor's open-work items.

- **"Report cut off" note:** the skeptic wrote that the auditor's report was cut off after the Redirects row. The full auditor evidence does classify S-M03-3 (already_fixed) and S-M03-6 (label fixed; money half still_present as P08-W3), and it agrees with the skeptic's rows.

### Notes for the lead

- **The auditor's "origin/main adds no public function" is superseded:** origin/main has since gained 4 public functions (83 at b6f5e1a).
- **The auditor's "the sweep would fail if any requireUserId were removed" is too strong:** it does not hold for no-id queries such as recovery.summary, which returns the same empty value either way.
- **Throwaway files:** every throwaway file used as evidence (`zz_paudit_p08_1..5`, `zz_paudit_p08s_1..2`) was deleted by its author. This section created only this file. The other untracked `zz_paudit_*` files in the worktree belong to other items' auditors.

## P09

**Account lifecycle: export, deletion, provider-resource deletion, disclosures.** Primary class: **still_present**.

- **Commit audited:** `a53a2bf` (main), in the worktree `recoup-wt-paudit`.
- **Passes:** an auditor pass and then an independent skeptic pass. Both are claude-opus-5-5. This section applies the skeptic's corrections.
- **Classification vocabulary** (mission §15): already_fixed, still_present, not_reproduced, changed_scope_with_evidence, externally_blocked.
- **"already_fixed"** is used only where a committed test fails when the fix is removed. The skeptic checked this by removing guards one at a time in a throwaway copy.
- **"not_reproduced"** is used for guards whose defect could not be reproduced (the guard works in a throwaway repro) but which no committed test proves. So a regression there would go unnoticed.

**Correction to the skeptic from this pass**
- **The skeptic's claim:** `src/lib/accountDeletion.test.ts:39-41` and `:43-49` "pin the false copy".
- **What those tests check:** `:39-41` asserts only that the copy contains "tombstone". `:43-49` forbids "not yet purge", "fix in progress", "known gap" and "continue to live". No test pins "nothing else".
- **What a truthful rewrite must still respect:**
  - It must avoid those four phrases.
  - It must keep the substrings "remote-inbox failure" and "cannot be recalled" that `:50-55` requires, or edit that test.
