#### P09-F1 (S-M03-2): shared sender inbox copies survive deletion; the deletion copy says "nothing else" (medium)
- **Description:** Sign-in and reset codes are sent by REST from `ALERTS_INBOX_ID` (`convex/lib/authMail.ts:168-188`), with no local join key. `mailEvents.ts:148` returns early on sent/delivered events. So their component events, which carry the recipient address, are never purged: not at deletion, and not by any retention job. The user's own replies to alert emails are stored as component `inboundMessages` in the shared inbox (`@agentmail/convex` `lib.ts:455-477`), and they are never purged either. Nothing deletes provider-side threads. Meanwhile `DELETION_WHAT_REMAINS` (`src/lib/accountDeletion.ts:34-38`) says "nothing else" remains, and the tombstone also keeps inboxId and lastError. Repros R1, R1b and X1: after a complete deletion plus 400 days of sweeps, `purgeInbox('inbox_alerts_shared')` still deletes 3 rows.
- **Smallest fix:**
  - (a) Now: rewrite `DELETION_WHAT_REMAINS` to state what the tombstone actually keeps: the account id, the Recoup inbox id, the deletion status and the last error. It should also say that the mail provider and the mail component keep copies of alert and sign-in-code emails sent from Recoup's shared sender mailbox, with their delivery records and replies. Keep "remote-inbox failure" and "cannot be recalled", or update `accountDeletion.test.ts:50-55`. Add a matching `PRIVACY_STATEMENTS.senderMailboxCopies` in `privacyFacts.ts`.
  - (b) Then: add a bounded daily sweep of `ALERTS_INBOX_ID` component rows (events and inboundMessages) by `by_inbox` plus age, with its window in privacyFacts (D133 N2). Alternatively, purge unmatched shared-inbox events by message id in `mailEvents.onEvent`.
- **Acceptance test:** invert R1/R1b/X1: after the sweep window, `purgeInbox('inbox_alerts_shared')` deletes 0 rows for the auth-code delivery event and the reply. `accountDeletion.test.ts` asserts the copy no longer contains "nothing else" and mentions the shared sender mailbox. `Privacy.test.tsx` renders the new statement exactly once.
- **Files:** `src/lib/accountDeletion.ts`, `src/lib/accountDeletion.test.ts`, `convex/lib/privacyFacts.ts`, `src/pages/Privacy.tsx`, `convex/mailEvents.ts` or `convex/mailPurge.ts` plus `convex/crons.ts`, `convex/mailEvents.test.ts` or `convex/account.test.ts`.
- **Externally blocked:** no, for the local fix. Deleting provider-side threads is part of P09-X1.

#### P09-F2: the Privacy provider disclosure is untrue or incomplete (medium)
- **Description:**
  - The OpenAI entry (`src/pages/Privacy.tsx:83-88`) says it receives "only that one message's text for that one extraction". In fact draft generation sends more (`drafts.ts:320-344`): the customer name (or the email local-part, `:256`), the order reference, item, prices, product URL and merchant-reply summaries. Product and policy page text is sent too (`priceWatch.ts:594-596`, `policies.ts:144`). Repro R3.
  - There is no model-provider retention statement, which SEC-DEL-5 requires.
  - The AgentMail entry (`:93-98`) omits sign-in and reset codes and the shared sender mailbox's retained copies.
  - `privacyFacts.mailComponentCopy` says "deleting your account purges it", which is false in X2 and X5.
  - The provider list is hand-written, not the backend constant that SEC-DEL-5 asks for.
- **Smallest fix:** add `PROVIDER_DISCLOSURES` to `convex/lib/privacyFacts.ts`. For each provider it holds the data categories received and a retention sentence. OpenAI gets one purpose entry per `extract()` purpose (intake, reply, price, policy, draft), and the draft entry names "your name and order details". The AgentMail entry mentions sign-in and reset codes and the shared sender mailbox. Render it verbatim in `Privacy.tsx`. Make `mailComponentCopy` conditional on the F3 and F4 fixes, or soften it.
- **Acceptance test:** a static test checks that every `extract("<name>", …)` call site name appears in `PROVIDER_DISCLOSURES.OpenAI.purposes`. `Privacy.test.tsx` renders every sentence exactly once. Invert R3: the page no longer contains "only that one message's text", and the AgentMail entry mentions sign-in codes.
- **Files:** `convex/lib/privacyFacts.ts`, `convex/lib/privacyFacts.test.ts`, `src/pages/Privacy.tsx`, `src/pages/Privacy.test.tsx`.
- **Externally blocked:** no. The exact provider retention window comes from provider documentation, which is part of X1.

#### P09-SK-1: OpenAI Responses calls omit `store`, and the provider stores responses by default (medium)
- **Description:**
  - `convex/lib/ai.ts:18-25` calls `responses.parse` without `store`.
  - `node_modules/openai/resources/responses/responses.d.ts:8970-8975` says `store` "Defaults to true when omitted". The same doc comment says stored data is kept for at least 30 days.
  - Every extraction is therefore retained at OpenAI and can be read back through the API. That covers inbound mail text, forwarded and pasted evidence (`intake.ts:329-334`, C56 row 13b), merchant replies, draft facts (customer name, order reference, prices) and page text.
  - Recoup records no response id, deletes none of these on account deletion, and discloses none of it.
  - Repro X8: the captured request body has no `store` key, and its input contains "Customer name: Jane Doe".
- **Smallest fix:** pass `store: false` in `extract()`, add a unit test, and add the OpenAI retention sentence from `PROVIDER_DISCLOSURES` (P09-F2).
- **Acceptance test:** with fetch stubbed, the `/responses` request body from `extract()` contains `"store": false`. The Privacy OpenAI entry states the model-provider retention window from a backend constant.
- **Files:** `convex/lib/ai.ts`, `convex/lib/ai.test.ts` (new or existing), `convex/lib/privacyFacts.ts`, `src/pages/Privacy.tsx`.
- **Externally blocked:** no. Verifying the provider-side behaviour is part of X1.

#### P09-SK-2: the final deletion outcome is never shown to the user or an operator (medium)
- **Description:**
  - `src/pages/Settings.tsx:222-224` signs the deleting tab out and navigates with `state: { accountDeleted: true }`, which nothing reads (`SignIn.tsx` has no `useLocation`).
  - Other tabs keep their JWT for at most 1 hour: `@convex-dev/auth` sets `DEFAULT_JWT_DURATION_MS` to 1h, `convex/auth.ts` sets no override, and refresh tokens are revoked at `account.ts:604`.
  - The final DELETE outcome lands about 7h11m later, so the "Failed after N attempts" and "Incomplete" branches (`Settings.tsx:545-566`) are unreachable.
  - The headline at `Settings.tsx:584` is unconditional.
  - `ops.ts:553` `deletions` counts only stuck 'deleting' rows.
  - `RUNBOOK.md:383-385` uses `take(20)`, and `:369-371` says 'deleted' means the component rows are fully gone.
  - All of this contradicts the promise in `DELETION_WHAT_REMAINS` that the failure "is shown rather than hidden".
- **Smallest fix:**
  - Add a bounded `deletions.deletedWithFailures` count to `ops.backlog`: 'deleted' rows with `inboxDeleted:false` or `mailDataPurged:false`, read through a `by_status` index page with a cap.
  - Document it in the RUNBOOK and fix the RUNBOOK's "fully gone" wording.
  - Reword the copy to say that failures are handled by the operator.
  - Either render `accountDeleted` on SignIn or remove it.
  - Make the DeletionInProgress headline conditional.
- **Acceptance test:** `ops.test.ts`: backlog reports 1 for a deleted tombstone with `inboxDeleted:false`, and 1 for one with `mailDataPurged:false`. `accountDeletion.test.ts`: the copy no longer promises a user-visible failure. A DOM test: the headline is not "removed" when `inboxDeleted:false`.
- **Files:** `convex/ops.ts`, `convex/ops.test.ts`, `docs/ops/RUNBOOK.md`, `src/lib/accountDeletion.ts`, `src/pages/Settings.tsx`, `src/pages/SignIn.tsx`.
- **Externally blocked:** no.

#### P09-F3: after the 5th failed remote DELETE the inbox stays live and is never retried or reported (low)
- **Description:**
  - The account is finalized as 'deleted' with `inboxDeleted:false`, but the inbox stays live at the provider.
  - Later mail is stored unmasked in the component for the deleted account. Repros R2 and X2 used a message.received containing "Card 4111 1111 1111 1111".
  - `deletionStatus` keeps saying `mailDataPurged:true`.
  - `reDriveStuckDeletions` rescheduled 0 and `stuckDeletions.stuck` is 0.
- **Smallest fix:**
  - Add a bounded daily cron over 'deleted' rows with `inboxDeleted:false` (an index or a capped `by_status` scan). Each run retries `inboxTransport.deleteInbox` at a slow cadence and re-runs `mailPurge.purgeInboxData`.
  - Report the count through P09-SK-2's `ops.backlog` field.
  - As a stopgap, `inbound.onMessageReceived` purges the component rows of any message addressed to a tombstoned inbox.
- **Acceptance test:** invert R2: after the late message.received and one cron run, `listInboundMessages({inboxId})` is empty. The backlog count stays 1 until a DELETE succeeds; then `inboxDeleted` becomes true.
- **Files:** `convex/account.ts`, `convex/crons.ts`, `convex/ops.ts`, `convex/schema.ts` (index), `convex/inbound.ts`, `convex/account.test.ts`, `convex/ops.test.ts`.
- **Externally blocked:** no for the mechanism. The live DELETE is part of X1.

#### P09-F4: an incomplete component purge is finalized and never resumed (low)
- **Description:** `mailPurge.purgeInboxData` can return `{complete:false}`, and its own docstring says the caller should then reschedule. Instead, `account.ts:1149-1154` records `mailDataPurged:false` and runs purgeAuth and finishPurge anyway. Repros R6 and X5: purgeInboxData is called once; the final state is `{status:'deleted', inboxDeleted:true, mailDataPurged:false}`; the re-drive picks up 0 rows; and `ops.backlog` has no field for this.
- **Smallest fix:** when `!mailResult.complete`, reschedule `purge` and keep the status 'deleting', bounded by a separate attempt counter. Finalize only once the purge is complete, or once the attempts are exhausted with `mailDataPurged:false`, and count that case in the P09-SK-2 backlog field.
- **Acceptance test:** mock `purgeInboxData` to return complete:false twice and then true. It is called 3 times, and the final state is `{status:'deleted', mailDataPurged:true}`. If it never completes, the attempts are bounded and backlog reports 1.
- **Files:** `convex/account.ts`, `convex/account.test.ts`, `convex/ops.ts`.
- **Externally blocked:** no.

#### P09-SK-7: the inbox-DELETE retry budget is shorter than documented (low)
- **Description:**
  - `account.ts:1128-1129` schedules a retry only while `attempts < 5` and uses `BACKOFF[attempts-1]`, so only 1m, 10m, 1h and 6h are used, about 7h11m in total.
  - The 24h entry at `account.ts:983` is dead code.
  - An AgentMail outage longer than about 7h therefore leaves the inbox orphaned permanently (the P09-F3 path).
  - `account.test.ts:1123` puts "24h" in its title but asserts 4 retries, and `docs/ops/RUNBOOK.md:373` states the 5-step schedule.
- **Smallest fix:** either raise `INBOX_DELETE_MAX_ATTEMPTS` to 6 so every backoff entry is used, or drop the 24h entry. In either case, make the test title and the RUNBOOK match the code.
- **Acceptance test:** five failing attempts produce retries at +1m, +10m, +1h, +6h and +24h. Otherwise, the test title and RUNBOOK state the 4-step schedule and the test asserts that no fifth retry is scheduled.
- **Files:** `convex/account.ts`, `convex/account.test.ts`, `docs/ops/RUNBOOK.md`.
- **Externally blocked:** no.

#### P09-SK-3: an ensureInbox call that straddles requestDeletion leaves a live orphan inbox (low)
- **Description:** ensureInbox's POST can complete after requestDeletion has committed. If the single compensating DELETE then fails (`profiles.ts:409-421`), the new inbox stays live, and its id is never stored on the tombstone. purge then finds no inboxId, sets `inboxDeleted:true` vacuously (`account.ts:1119`), and Settings shows "Remote inbox: Deleted". Repro X3.
- **Smallest fix:** when `save()` returns null because the user is tombstoned, write the just-created inboxId onto `accountState` (or a list field) so that purge's existing retry chain deletes it. Never report `inboxDeleted:true` for an inbox that was created but not confirmed deleted.
- **Acceptance test:** ensureInbox straddles requestDeletion and the compensating `deleteInbox` fails. The orphan inboxId ends up on `accountState`, purge retries it on its backoff, and `inboxDeleted` stays false until a DELETE succeeds.
- **Files:** `convex/profiles.ts`, `convex/account.ts`, `convex/account.test.ts` or `convex/profiles.test.ts`.
- **Externally blocked:** no.

#### P09-SK-4: a late inbound webhook leaves a processedEvents row owned by the deleted user (low)
- **Description:** `inbound.ts:125-159` inserts the processedEvents row and sets `userId` before its tombstone check. `PURGE_STEPS` (`account.ts:188`) removes profiles after processedEvents. So a webhook that lands between those two purge steps leaves a row that carries the user's id and the mail text after the status is 'deleted'. Retention clears only the payload, after 30 days. Repro X4.
- **Smallest fix:** when the owner is tombstoned, `inbound.onMessageReceived` clears the payload and leaves `userId` unset. Alternatively, `finishPurge` re-runs one processedEvents page.
- **Acceptance test:** in lifecycle, a webhook delivered between the processedEvents and profiles purge steps leaves no processedEvents row with that userId or mail text once the status is 'deleted'.
- **Files:** `convex/inbound.ts`, `convex/account.ts`, `convex/lifecycle.test.ts`.
- **Externally blocked:** no.

#### P09-F5 (S-M03-7): carried Mission-1 LOW residuals (low)
- **Description:** unchanged since `357dc37`:
  - F-T18.6-1: `account.ts:716-733` skips a mailLog row that is still 'remaining' after 5 attempts, and that row keeps the user's address. `account.test.ts:1637` asserts this.
  - N1: `mailPurge.ts:99-108` and the component's `cleanupFinalizedOutbound` take 200 rows per status, with no resumable budget.
  - N4: `profiles.ts:241-254` releaseProvisioning is not compare-and-clear, and `profiles.ts:291` createInboxRemote and `account.ts:1000` deleteInbox have no fetch timeout.
  - F-T18.6-2 is a deliberate throttle and is not work.
- **Smallest fix:** as D133 prescribes:
  - `AbortSignal.timeout(…)` on both fetches.
  - Make releaseProvisioning compare-and-clear on `provisioningAt`.
  - A per-sweep delete budget with a cursor for `cleanupFinalizedOutbound`.
  - A structured log plus an `ops.backlog` count for skipped mailLog rows.
- **Acceptance test:**
  - A hung fetch stub makes `deleteInbox` reject within the timeout, and that counts as a failed attempt.
  - A stale `releaseProvisioning` leaves a newer claim alone.
  - `account.test.ts:1637` is changed to assert that the skipped row appears in `ops.backlog`.
- **Files:** `convex/profiles.ts`, `convex/account.ts`, `convex/mailPurge.ts`, `convex/ops.ts`, related tests.
- **Externally blocked:** no.

#### P09-F6 (narrowed after the skeptic): the export copy is inaccurate (low)
- **Description:** `Settings.tsx:456-458` lists only Mission-1 tables (carried from D187). Both it and `Privacy.tsx:151` say the export is "everything … as one JSON file". But uploaded file bytes are only listed, by id, contentHash, fileName and hasFile. The users row is not exported either; that part is fixed under SK-5(b).
- **Smallest fix:** build the Settings export sentence from a shared constant naming the `EXPORT_TABLES` categories. Replace "everything … as one JSON file" with wording that says uploaded files are listed by name and fingerprint, not included.
- **Acceptance test:** a component test asserts that the Settings export copy names every `EXPORT_TABLES` category and mentions uploaded files. `Privacy.test.tsx` asserts the same for the Privacy sentence.
- **Files:** `src/pages/Settings.tsx`, `src/lib/accountExport.ts`, `src/pages/Privacy.tsx`, a new Settings export-copy test.
- **Externally blocked:** no.

#### P09-SK-5: export gaps (low)
- **Description:**
  - (a) `src/lib/accountExport.ts:103` caps every table at 500 pages. processedEvents and evidence are paged 25 rows at a time, and retention never deletes those rows. So a user with more than about 12,500 such rows can never export: the whole export aborts (repro X6). The inbound limit of 60 per hour per inbox reaches that in about 9 days.
  - (b) The export has no account section (email, name, `_creationTime`). Yet `Privacy.tsx:151` says it covers "every table listed under Providers above", and that list includes the account (`Privacy.tsx:75-80`).
- **Smallest fix:** bound the export by rows, not pages, or scale the page cap by the server page size. Add a small owner-only `account` section to `exportPage`.
- **Acceptance test:** 12,600 processedEvents rows export completely, and the export document includes the account's email and name.
- **Files:** `src/lib/accountExport.ts`, `src/lib/accountExport.test.ts`, `convex/account.ts`, `convex/account.test.ts`.
- **Externally blocked:** no.

#### P09-F7 (merged with P09-SK-6): guards and a status mapping with no committed test that fails on removal (low)
- **Description:**
  - No committed test fails when any one of these guards is removed alone:
    - `evidence.ts:320`, the tombstone gate on `GET /evidence/file`
    - `evidence.ts:417-420`, the finalizeUpload race
    - `opportunities.ts:343`, evaluateTransaction
    - either layer of the exportPage via-parent IDOR defence (`account.ts:397`, and `:431`/`:451`)
  - `lifecycle.test.ts:237` does not test the tombstone: it passes with the `requireUserId` check removed.
  - The Settings DeletionInProgress mapping (`remoteInboxStatus`, `storedMailCopiesStatus` and the headline at `:584`) has no test.
- **Smallest fix:** add these focused tests, each confirmed to fail against a copy with its single guard removed:
  - A tombstoned `GET /evidence/file` returns 401/404.
  - finalizeUpload after requestDeletion is refused and the blob is gone (R5).
  - evaluateTransaction for a tombstoned owner writes nothing (R7).
  - A forged cursor is refused with only isOwnedParent in place, and again with only the child filter in place.
  - `lifecycle:237` asserts the tombstone error message.
  - A DOM test covers DeletionInProgress in the pending, failed, incomplete and deleted states.
- **Acceptance test:** each guard, removed alone, fails at least one committed test. The DOM test fails if "Incomplete" or "Failed after N attempts" is rendered as success.
- **Files:** `convex/lifecycle.test.ts`, `convex/evidence.test.ts`, `convex/account.test.ts`, `src/pages/Settings.deletion.test.tsx` (new).
- **Externally blocked:** no.

#### P09-F8: suppressQueuedMail never cancels the component send (low; race not reproduced)
- **Description:** `account.ts:553-564` only relabels queued alerts as suppressed/deleted. It never calls `components.agentmail.lib.cancelSend`, so it relies on the purge's mailLog step removing the pending outbound row before the workpool's `performSend` runs. In convex-test the purge won (R4: 0 POSTs). Under a workpool backlog, an alert could still reach a user whose deletion is in progress while the row says suppressed.
- **Smallest fix:** for each queued row that has an `outboundId`, call `cancelSend` through a `mailPurge` wrapper that swallows the not-pending case, then relabel the row.
- **Acceptance test:** with a queued alert and requestDeletion committed, `getOutboundStatus` is 'failed' ("Cancelled by user") before any scheduled function runs, and 0 POSTs follow.
- **Files:** `convex/account.ts`, `convex/mailPurge.ts`, `convex/lifecycle.test.ts`.
- **Externally blocked:** no.

#### P09-D1: doc drift (low)
- **Description:** `docs/team/CONNECTIONS.md:200` still lists C56 as NOT_IMPLEMENTED, although M14 landed (D163). `docs/ops/RUNBOOK.md:369-373` states the 5-step backoff schedule and says 'deleted' means the component rows are fully gone. Both statements are false (SK-7, F4).
- **Smallest fix:** mark C56 partially implemented (storage and jobs done; provider leg per rows 13a-13d). Fix the RUNBOOK together with SK-7 and SK-2.
- **Acceptance test:** doc review. Once SK-2 lands, the RUNBOOK names the new `ops.backlog` field instead of the `take(20)` query.
- **Files:** `docs/team/CONNECTIONS.md`, `docs/ops/RUNBOOK.md`.
- **Externally blocked:** no.

#### P09-X1: live provider deletion and retention cannot be verified (low; externally blocked)
- **Description:** none of the following can be verified here: the live AgentMail inbox DELETE, remote thread deletion in the shared inbox, AgentMail retention of sent mail, and OpenAI API data retention (with and without `store:false`). Dev has placeholder keys (D83), and production is not authorized (D136). The local mechanisms are verified above.
- **Smallest fix:** when a real key and an authorized target exist, run one deletion end to end and check the provider APIs for the inbox, its threads and the stored responses. Record the provider retention terms from their documentation into the privacyFacts constants (F2, SK-1).
- **Acceptance test:** a smoke deletion on an authorized deployment: `GET /inboxes/<id>` returns 404; `deletionStatus` is `{status:'deleted', inboxDeleted:true, mailDataPurged:true}`; and no stored OpenAI response exists for that run.
- **Files:** `scripts/smoke.mjs`, `docs/ops/RUNBOOK.md`.
- **Externally blocked:** **yes** (D83, D136).

### Refuted

- **P09-F6 as the auditor originally scoped it: an export defect because uploaded file bytes are left out and no page calls `/evidence/file`.** The skeptic refuted the severity, not the fact.
  - On main, `src/` has no upload or download path at all; grep finds no `/evidence/upload` or `/evidence/file`.
  - So no user of the web app can own an uploaded file that the export leaves out. Only raw-HTTP API uploads are affected.
  - The "add a files step to the export" and "frontend download" parts are dropped until the upload UI lands, which is M23/M24 scope.
  - The copy-accuracy part is kept as P09-F6 (narrowed).

### Tests and repros behind this section

- **Auditor:**
  - It ran 27 test files: 550/550 pass. That was 146 across account, lifecycle, mailPurge, mailEvents, accountState, privacyFacts, blobRefs, accountDeletion, Privacy and accountExport; 311 across the auth, notify, alerts, retention, http, crons, ops, evidence, access and authMail tests; and 93 across facts.reevaluate, isolationM1, drafts and followUps.
  - Its throwaway repros R1-R7 reproduce every still_present item and confirm the R4, R5 and R7 behaviour. It deleted them afterwards.
- **Skeptic:**
  - Its throwaway repros X1-X8 all pass on main.
  - It ran mutation tests in a scratch copy: account, lifecycle and isolationM1 102/102; evidence, isolationM1 and http 78/78; and 251 + 140 + 151 + 78 tests across the Mission-2 suites, all with single guards removed. It deleted all of it afterwards.
- **This pass:**
  - `npx vitest run src/lib/accountDeletion.test.ts convex/lib/privacyFacts.test.ts src/pages/Privacy.test.tsx` passed 3 files, 20/20.
  - I re-read the cited lines for the backoff arithmetic (`account.ts:983-984`, `:1128-1129`), `lib/ai.ts:18-25` (no `store`), the `responses.d.ts:8970-8975` default, `Settings.tsx:224` (the only `accountDeleted` reference), `Settings.tsx:584`, `accountDeletion.test.ts:38-55`, `ops.ts:553`, `RUNBOOK.md:369-385` and `CONNECTIONS.md:200`. All match the findings.
- **Worktree:** this pass created only this file. The untracked `zz_paudit_*` files in the worktree (`P09_1..5`, `p06s`, `p10k`) belong to other agents and were not touched.

## P10

**Scope.** Browser acceptance and resilience:
- the Phase 1 flows exercised in a real browser;
- keyboard use and accessibility;
- failure, offline, missing-config and expired-session states;
- traces;
- loading and bundle size;
- whether the CI e2e job actually runs;
- whether the suite is deterministic at `--retries=0`.

**Overall: still_present.** Audited on main `a53a2bf`, in the worktree `recoup-wt-paudit`. `origin/main` has moved since (up to `b6f5e1a`); the "Upstream drift" section below lists what that changes for P10. Nothing in this section was re-run at `origin/main`.

**Basis.**
- **Auditor (claude-opus-5-5).**
  - Ran `r01-opportunity.spec.ts` plus the other 7 spec files on the desktop-chromium project only, at `--retries=0`, against `adorable-lion-138`.
    - The first attempt gave 27/30, with 3 transient failures.
    - An immediate rerun of the affected tests gave 4/4.
  - Also ran:
    - component and convex-test files, 80/80, 29/29 and 15/15;
    - a scratchpad Playwright config that checks which artifacts are written on failure;
    - a `vite build`;
    - five throwaways (`zz_paudit_p10_1..5`, all deleted).
  - All of these passed.
- **Skeptic (claude-opus-5-5).**
  - Changed 5 sub-requirement classes, re-confirmed 4, added 8 missed items and refuted 1 re-cited finding.
  - Its throwaways (`zz_paudit_p10sk_1..4`, deleted) all passed.
  - It also ran `drafts.prepare` + `Composer` + `isolationM1` + `testing.seeders`: 96/96.
- **This write-up (claude-opus-5-5).**
  - Spot-read every load-bearing line cited below.
  - Re-ran `npx vitest run convex/isolationM1.test.ts convex/testing.seeders.test.ts convex/drafts.prepare.test.ts src/components/claim/Composer.test.tsx`: **4 files, 96/96 passed**.
  - Independently confirmed the skeptic's most important new finding (MW-2) with the throwaway `convex/zz_paudit_P10_w1.test.ts` (1/1 passed, deleted). With `fetch` stubbed to an OpenAI-shaped 401, `api.drafts.generate` rejects with a plain `Error` whose message contains "Incorrect API key provided". It is not a `ConvexError`.
  - Did not re-run Playwright: the shared dev deployment is already loaded by other agents, and deploying is a lead action.
  - Did not read any secret. The statement that dev's Firecrawl key is real comes from `docs/team/CONNECTIONS.md:156`.

### Refuted

- **F-AUD-11, re-cited in the auditor's traces row and OW-4 ("ci.yml:223 does not pin `--workers=1`").**
  - Refuted: `playwright.config.ts:37` sets `workers: 1`, and `ci.yml:223` runs a plain `npx playwright test`, so CI already uses one worker (both re-checked).
  - Only someone passing `--workers>1` by hand can race the shared lead account (F-T23-2), and `e2e/README.md` already warns about that.
  - Removed from OW-4.
- **Traces/screenshots as still_present.**
  - Refuted: at `--retries=0` the repo's `use` block writes a screenshot, a video and `error-context.md` for every failing test. Only `trace.zip` is limited to the first retry.
  - The acceptance ("trace/screenshot artifacts on failure") holds, so the row is reclassified as changed_scope_with_evidence (row 18).
  - OW-4 survives as a low-severity diagnostic-depth item.
- **Loading/bundle as still_present.**
  - Refuted: the bundle is split and measured. At `a53a2bf` the build gives index 61.46 kB with no >500 kB warning, meeting `PLAN.md:151`.
  - CI publishes sizes, and app-ready timings are logged. The only gap is the missing budget gate, which is scope (OW-6).
  - Reclassified as changed_scope_with_evidence (row 19). The real loading defect is MW-1.

### Merged, not refuted

- **MW-3 → OW-5.** The skeptic said the auditor had no row for "deterministic browser tests". In fact the auditor had row 24 and OW-5. The skeptic's causes and fix (`gotoReady`, `failOnFlakyTests`, severity medium) are folded into OW-5.
- **MW-8 → OW-10.** The skeptic said QA-M16-4 was only a caveat. The auditor did carry it as OW-10. The skeptic's hour-by-hour evidence (refused at 10:30 and 11:30 UTC; accepted at 09:30, 12:30 and 20:30; US zones unaffected) is folded in.
- **The skeptic's MW-2 severity (medium) stands.** It is independently re-confirmed in this write-up (`convex/zz_paudit_P10_w1.test.ts`, 1/1, deleted).

## P11

**Scope:** pinned runtime, reproducible install, typecheck, lint, tests, build, codegen drift, isolated browser checks, the version-bound AgentMail patch, and no weakening of types or lint rules.

**Audited at:** main `a53a2bf` (worktree `recoup-wt-paudit`). Newer main was checked read-only up to `b6f5e1a`.

**Primary class:** `still_present`.

**Passes:** auditor and skeptic both completed (Opus 5.5 / claude-opus-5-5, self-reported). The statuses below include the skeptic's two corrections.

**Proof standard:** "already_fixed" names the test or CI mechanism that would fail if the fix were removed. Where the only proof was a throwaway test that has since been deleted, the row says so and points to the open work that would commit a durable test.

### Refuted

The skeptic refuted none of the auditor's open-work items (W1–W11). It did refute two hypotheses raised during the skeptic pass:

- **"localeshift does not reach the server tests"** (edge-runtime VM, or the D205 DOM pin, neutralising it). Refuted: under `LC_ALL=de_DE.UTF-8`, an edge-runtime vitest worker resolves `de-DE` and formats `1.234,50 $`; without it, it resolves `en-US` and `$1,234.50`. The en-US pin in `src/test/domLocale.setup.ts:11-13` applies only when `document` exists (D205). Throwaway `zz_paudit_P11s_1`, deleted.
- **"The 3c46cc4 localeshift failure is a locale bug."** Refuted: the same test failed with the same error in the en-US checks job at 364ac39, while clockshift passed. It is a timing flake, tracked as P11-MW1.

- Every P11 throwaway file (`zz_paudit_P11_1..4`, `zz_paudit_P11s_1`) and scratch dir was deleted.
- No production files were edited, and nothing was committed, pushed, stashed or deployed.
- The only file this section adds is `docs/reviews/_paudit-sections/P11.md`.
- Untracked `zz_paudit_P09_*`, `p06s_*` and `p10k_*` files in the worktree belong to other concurrent auditors and were not touched.

## P12

**Item:** operational release readiness. This covers the deploy target and gated path, revision and build identity, the configuration and provider matrix, rule-pack versions, stale docs, redacted diagnostics, operator kill switches, the release manifest, the environment checklist, migration, backup and restore, deployment order, smoke, rollback limits and the patch gate.

**Baseline:** main at `a53a2bf`, audited in `/Users/nihalnihalani/Desktop/Github/recoup-wt-paudit`.

**Overall: still_present.** The mechanisms that exist are sound and tested: pause/resume, approval-gated flags, fail-closed providers, the rule-pack gate, bounded diagnostics and gated `deploy:dev`. The release and operator layer around them has not caught up with Mission 2:

- the documented backup silently drops uploaded evidence;
- the documented rollback cannot be pushed once Mission 2 data exists;
- `ops.backlog` throws at the read limit during an inbound-failure burst;
- `npm run deploy` is an ungated production push;
- there is no Mission 2 manifest, build id or revision.

**How this section was made:**

- **Auditor:** Opus 5.5. 13 throwaway repro tests (R1–R14) and 3 read-limit repros (R13, R13b, R13c).
- **Skeptic:** Opus 5.5. Mutation runs in a `git archive` copy of HEAD, plus seven throwaway repro files. All auditor and skeptic throwaway files were deleted.
- **Re-check while writing this section:** `npx vitest run convex/ops.test.ts convex/http.test.ts convex/opportunities.paths.test.ts convex/migrations.test.ts convex/testing/checkRulePacks.test.ts` gave 5 files, 111/111 passed. I also spot-checked these lines:
  - `package.json:25` (the ungated `deploy` script);
  - `RUNBOOK.md:296-302` and `RELEASE.md:69` (export without `--include-file-storage`);
  - `RELEASE.md:176-179`;
  - `ops.ts:69-88` (pause writes only today's UTC row);
  - `ops.ts:433-435`, `ops.ts:449-450`, `ops.ts:599-602`;
  - `limits.ts:132` (`draft_generate` has no `global`);
  - `README.md:67/82/95`, `ENVIRONMENT.md:66`, `INSTALL.md:70/87`;
  - 10 registrations in `crons.ts`.

  The skeptic's corrections are applied below.

### Refuted

- **Source refresh as still_present.** The skeptic reclassified it as changed_scope_with_evidence. The only active pack (R01 v1) pins no refreshable source (`sources: []`, manifest `refreshDays` null), so the unwired `ruleSourceInputs()` hides nothing today, and backlog reports `inputsWired:false` honestly (skeptic `zz_p12s_sources` 2/2). The work item stays as W5 at low severity, reframed as a guard that blocks activating a sourced pack until it is wired.
- **`http.test.ts` F-T22-1 as the test that proves the `http.ts:37` guard.** A mutation run deleted the guard and `http.test.ts` stayed 15/15. The captured component secret plus the `http.ts:51` catch still return the empty 401. The behaviour does fail closed, but the proof does not exist. Row 5 was downgraded, and W12 now carries the pinning test.
- **R13c (evidence-row variant of the backlog read-limit wedge).** Not reachable today. Text evidence is written with `extractionStatus: 'succeeded'` (`evidence.ts:302`), uploads carry no text (the M23 PDF text layer has not landed), and live extraction is OFF (D145). So `backlog.extraction` reads only small rows. W2 keeps a small evidence cap only as defence in depth. The processedEvents variant is real, and worse than the auditor reported, since it reproduces with plain ASCII payloads.
- **"No deployment-side query reports which pack versions are active".** Overstated. `opportunities.forTransaction`/`forPurchase` (`opportunities.ts:779-830`) return `pathsNotChecked` with the ruleId and version of each active pack to the signed-in owner. Row 7 stays still_present only because there is no operator or internal query and the manifest omits pack versions. This is covered by W6.

### Worktree note

The only file I created is this one, `docs/reviews/_paudit-sections/P12.md`, together with its directory. Nothing is committed. The throwaway P12 repros from the auditor and skeptic were deleted before this pass. The `zz_paudit_*` files still untracked in the worktree belong to other items (P06, P09, P10).

## Consolidated backlog

This is every open item from the twelve sections, sorted by severity: high, medium, low, then info. Within each severity, items follow P order, with externally blocked items last.

- **Duplicates.** Where two or more items describe the same defect, they are merged into one row. The row's ID lists every source item: the first ID is the lead row, and the others follow as `(+…)`.
- **Related items.** Items that overlap without being duplicates stay separate. The list after the tables links them.
- **Severity.** It is the section's own after the skeptic's corrections. A merged row takes the highest severity of its sources.
- **Owner lanes.** backend = M20/M29/M2C, ingestion-integrations = M23/M28, frontend = M24/M2A, qa = M25.

**Totals:** 175 section items, which become 164 after merging 11 duplicates: 3 high, 54 medium, 106 low and 1 info. 10 of them are externally blocked.

- **Timeout pattern:** P02-OW-6 (the component's send fetch, patched in the AgentMail patch) and P08-W6 (direct provider fetches). Same fix pattern, different code.
- **Boundary tests:** P08-W4 is the reflective sweep over public functions. P09-F7 covers HTTP-route and internal guards and the two export layers. They complement each other.
- **Flaky tests in CI:** P10-OW-5 and P11-W7 both set `failOnFlakyTests` or `--retries=0` for CI.
- **Deployed revision:** P12-W6 (manifest and backlog revision) and P10-MW-7 (`testing:buildInfo`). One mechanism can serve both.
- **Revoked sessions:** P10-OW-9 (a revoked JWT still works for up to 1 h) and P01-5 (no test of reset session revocation).
- **New `ops.backlog` fields:** P09-SK-2 and P12-W7. Design them together.
- **`it.todo` passes CI:** P11-W1 (skip and todo counted as passed) and P08-W2.
- **Market refresh:** P03-A (no refresh UI) and P07-W5 (the newest points are unreadable once refresh ships). Land the read-order fix first.
- **RUNBOOK §3 migrations:** P03-SK-4 (`migrateStamps` cannot be re-run) and P12-S-4 (`linkLegacyPurchases` missing).
- **Privacy page copy:** P01-4 and P09-F2 cover different sentences of `Privacy.tsx`. Do them in one copy pass.
- **Byte-safe `processedEvents` reads:** P07-W1 and P12-W2. The structural fix (moving the payload into its own table) closes both.
- **QA-M16-4 residuals:** P06-OW-3r and P10-OW-10.
- **Shared live rehearsal:** P01-7, P02-OW-8, P08-EXT-1, P09-X1, P10-OW-12b and P12-W13 all need real AgentMail or OpenAI keys on an authorized deployment. P03-X and P04-OW12 both need one live ShopSavvy capture. One authorized rehearsal window per provider can close most of them.
